closely correlated assets and then we're waiting for a swing point to form and the candle that forms a swing point must be a different closure amongst the assets so a bullish swing point forms let's say at midnight we're then looking for that midnight candle to close bullish on one asset for example NQ and then that midnight candle must close bearish on another closely correlated asset for example ES okay so the six-hour time frame condenses each daily cycle quarter into a singular candle since PSPs help us identify reversals and continuations spotting one lets us anticipate the next 6-hour candles move revealing where the next daily session quarter is likely to go so as we know PSPs you know are usually there before reversals or at least continuation moves higher or lower if we spot a PSP on that 6-hour time frame it shows us that that session is likely the start of a reversal so say for example the midnight candle on the 6-hour time frame forms as a PSP we can then expect
